Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The nlp operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 245. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 340 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the nlp space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 245 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 340. QED.
